## 0.1 AAA MEMORY DOCTRINE & LOW-LEVEL PIPELINE (GEMINI INJECTED)
Before executing any block, the executor MUST comply with `AETHEL_AAA_SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md`.
1.  **Zero-Allocation Hot Loops**: `Vec::new()` and `String` are banned in `SimulationTick`. Use `LinearFrameAllocator`.
2.  **SoA Cache-Alignment**: All ECS components MUST be packed in Structure-of-Arrays (SoA) and padded to 16/64 byte cache-line boundaries via `#[repr(C, align(X))]`.
3.  **Zero-Copy FFI**: Data transferred between Rust and Tauri/WebGPU must use `memmap2` or `SharedArrayBuffer`. No serialization.

**Floor (#72):** Public launch locked until **100%+ holistic UE5 parity and absolute supremacy** — P1 Rendering + P2 GAS/Physics + P3 World Forge + P4 Workforce AI acceptance-green **together** in one integrated scene. **G.% ladder alone is NOT the entire gate.**
